## Document 006: 115年9月全國賦稅收入初步統計 (2026-10-13)

Source PDF: all report pdf/115年9月全國賦稅收入初步統計 (2026-10-13).pdf
Report source: MOF
Date: 2026-10-13
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

網際網路 https://www.mof.gov.tw同步發送 
下次發布日期：115 年 11 月 11 日下午 4 時

聯 絡 人： 陳 巧 芸 科 長 
電   話：02-2322-7555 
115年 9月全國賦稅收入初步統計 
一、全國賦稅收入 
（一）本月數 
9 月實徵淨額 4,965 億元，較上年同月增加 813 億元(+19.6%)。 
（二）累計數 
累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額 3 兆 5,714 億元，較上年同期增加 7,004 億
元(+24.4%)。 
（三）達成率 
1.中央政府總預算業於 9 月 18 日公布，稅課收入較原預算案提高
6,000 億元，併計地方政府及特種基金後，全國稅課收入較預算案
提高 5,852 億元，本次發布均改依法定數計算預算達成率(實徵淨
額占全年預算數)。 
2.累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額占累計分配預算數 115.0%，占全年預算數
78.8%。 
圖 1 全國賦稅收入及年增率

說明：115 年 5-7 月年增率跳動較大，係因 114 年所得稅結算申報及繳納期限延長至 6 月底所致。

### Page 2

二、主要稅目別變動
（一）9 月實徵淨額與上年同月比較，以營業稅增加 297 億元、營利事業
所得稅增加 254 億元及證券交易稅增加 222 億元較多。
（二）累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額與上年同期比較，以證券交易稅增加 3,077
億元、營利事業所得稅增加 1,853 億元及營業稅增加 1,203 億元較多。
圖 2 主要稅目別變化
(115 年 1-9 月)
1.營利事業所得稅
(1)9 月實徵淨額 2,007 億元，較上年同月增加 254 億元(+14.5%)，主
因暫繳稅款成長。
(2)累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額 1 兆 28 億元，較上年同期增加 1,853 億元
(+22.7%)，主因結算申報自繳 、暫繳稅款 俱增及延分期稅款陸續
入帳。
2.綜合所得稅
9 月實徵淨額 490 億元，較上年同月增加 34 億元(+7.4%)，主因扣繳
稅款增加所致；累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額 8,279 億元，較上年同期增加
790 億元(+10.6%)，主因結算申報自繳增加，以及盈餘分配、薪資所

得等扣繳稅款均呈成長。
3.營業稅
9月實徵淨額1,414億元，較上年同月增加297億元(+26.6%)，累計1至
9月實徵淨額6,230億元，較上年同期增加1,203億元(+23.9%)，均因內
需穩定，加以電子零組件 、資通與視聽產品 等進口代徵稅額增加 ，
惟出口暢旺，適用外銷零稅率營業人申請退稅亦增，抵銷部分增幅。

### Page 3

3
4.貨物稅
(1)9月實徵淨額100億元，較上年同月減少11億元(-10.0%)，主因汽柴
油減徵幅度擴大，惟電動車大量到港推升進口 車稅額，抵銷部分
減幅。
(2)累計1至9月實徵淨額874億元，較上年同期減少207億元(-19.2%)，
除汽柴油降稅因素外 ，亦與新購汽機車及電動車之減免徵退稅增
加有關。
5.證券交易稅
(1)9 月及累計 1 至 9 月上市上櫃股票平均每日成交值為 1 兆 317 億
元、1 兆 1,461 億元，分別較上年同期(6,006 億元、4,451 億元)增
71.8%、1.6 倍。
(2)9 月 證 交 稅 實 徵 淨 額534 億 元， 較 上 年 同 月 增 加 222 億 元
(+71.2%)；累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額 5,060 億元，較上年同期增加
3,077 億元(+1.6 倍)。
6.關稅
9 月實徵淨額 157 億元，較上年同月增加 3 億元(+1.9%)，累計 1 至 9
月實徵淨額 1,224 億元，較上年同期增加 80 億元(+7.0%)，均因機械

及電機設備、基本金屬及其製品等進口稅額增加。
7.贈與稅
9 月實徵淨額 23 億元，較上年同月增加 6 億元(+35.7%)，累計 1 至 9
月實徵淨額 211 億元，較上年同期增加 43 億元(+25.3%)，主因大額
稅款案件增加。
8.土地增值稅
9 月實徵淨額 56 億元，較上年同月增加 12 億元(+26.6%)，主因大額
稅款案件增加；累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額 504 億元，較上年同期減少
14 億元(-2.8%)，主因房地交易量及大額稅款案件減少。
9.印花稅
9 月實徵淨額 31 億元，較上年同月增加 4 億元(+16.2%)，累計 1 至 9
月實徵淨額 175 億元，較上年同期增加 15 億元(+9.0%)，主因銀行放
款稅額成長。
（三）前 9月主要稅目實徵淨額占分配預算數之比率：
1.大於 100%者有證交稅(272%)、遺產稅(155%)、贈與稅(132%)、印花
稅(130%)、營業稅(116%)、關稅(114%)、契稅(111%)、房屋稅(106%)、

綜所稅(101%)、牌照稅(101%)及營所稅(101%)等。
2.低於 100%者有貨物稅(98%)、菸酒稅(97%)、土增稅(85%)、地價稅
(84%)及特銷稅(57%)等。

### Page 4

4
三、各級政府賦稅收入概況
(一)中央政府
累計 1 至 9 月中央政府賦稅收入 (不含中央統籌分配稅款 )實徵淨 額
2 兆 3,603 億元(占分配預算數 119.3%)，較上年同期增加 2,553 億元
(+12.1%)，雖新版財政收支劃分法 開始施行，營業稅劃歸中央政府 比
率大幅調降，減少 2,748 億元(-90.7%)，惟證交稅、所得稅分別增加 3,077
億元、2,230 億元，互抵後呈現上升。
(二)地方政府
累計 1 至 9 月地方政府賦稅收
入(含中央統籌分配稅款 )實徵
淨額 1 兆 898 億元(占分配預
算數 109.4%)，較上年同期增
加 4,527 億元(+71.0%)，主因
納入中央統籌分配 之營業稅
增加 4,030 億元(+2.1 倍)所致。
(三)中央特種基金
累計 1 至 9 月撥入中央特種基金之健康福利捐實徵淨額 195 億元，較
上年同期增加 2 億元(+0.8%)；房地合一課徵所得稅撥入住宅基金 135
億元，較上年同期減少 7 億元(-4.7%)；撥入長照基金 883 億元(其中 541

億元來自房地合一課徵所得稅、195 億元來自菸稅，146 億元來自遺贈
稅)，較上年同期增加 8 億元(+0.9%)。

価格指数の上昇は企業のコスト負担増を示唆

ユーロ圏総合PMIは節目の50超を維持。小売売上
高も小幅増加。ドイツの鉱工業生産は持ち直したが、
製造業受注は大型案件の反動で減少。景気は緩
やかな回復を維持するも、持続力には不透明感
欧
州
ASEAN経済は国ごとにばらつき。ベトナムは輸出や
鉱工業生産の好調を背景に高成⾧持続。フィリピン
では食料品やエネルギー価格高騰が消費の重石に。
タイでも原油高を背景にインフレ圧力が強まる
APAC
賃金改善が続く一方、個人消費の回復に弱さ。実
質消費支出は前年比減少が継続。消費者マインド

も小幅悪化し、物価高への警戒が根強い。景気動
向指数は低下したものの、基調判断は改善を維持
日
本
先週（10/5～10/12）発表の主な経済指標
米国では金融政策運営の変化と財政懸念、欧州では財政不
安が金利上昇圧力に。欧州では⾧期金利上昇による景気下
押し圧力が、ECBの追加利上げの必要性を低下させる可能性
金利・コスト上昇などの逆風下でも、設備投資意欲は旺盛。家
計の節約志向や企業間の格差は残るものの、価格転嫁・賃上
げも継続しており、日銀の想定するシナリオに沿った動き
注目点② 日銀さくらレポートは企業活動の底堅さを再確認注目点①米欧で金利上昇。異なる背景と金融政策への含意

期待より悪い 期待通り 期待より良い
（シェア）
米国：中間選挙を目前に住宅市場は軟調、アフォーダビリティへの不満が共和党への逆風に
 住宅ローン金利は足元で再び上昇
― 借入コストの負担増により、足元の住宅ローン申請件数は減少
 物価上昇が生活コストを押し上げるなか、金利上昇による住宅取得環境の悪化も家計の不満を助長する要因に
― トランプ大統領の生活費への政策対応は約7割が「期待より悪い」と評価しており、支持率は低下基調
―11月に控える中間選挙では、アフォーダビリティへの対応が引き続き主要な論点

 フランスでは国内政治・財政面の不安から長期金利が上昇。イタリア・ギリシャなど周辺の高債務国にも波及
― フランス国債の対独利回り差は急拡大し、一時欧州債務危機（2012年）以来の水準に。その後、大統領選の有力候補と

―ECBによる介入（国債買い入れ等）の可能性については、複数の高官が検討段階にないとの見方を示す

日本：金利・コスト上昇下でも企業活動は底堅く、設備投資意欲の強さを再確認
 10月のさくらレポートではAI関連需要の拡大とその裾野の広がりを背景に、企業活動の底堅さを確認
― 同一の企業コメントに登場するテーマの組み合わせを集計した共起分析では、「金利×設備投資」の組み合わせが増加。
金利上昇への意識が強まる一方、成長・省人化投資の旺盛なニーズは継続
 賃上げや資産効果が消費を下支えする一方、物価高を背景とした消費の二極化が継続
― 企業は価格転嫁を積極化するも、消費者の節約志向が根強く、商品競争力や顧客層によって転嫁余力に格差
⇒景気・物価は強弱入り混じるも、日銀の想定するシナリオに概ね沿った展開。12月利上げ見通しは不変

### Page 9

世界経済：エネルギー・食料の供給ショックによる負担は、所得階層や国によって格差
 生活費高騰に伴うインフレの影響は所得階層により格差
― 食料・エネルギー支出の割合が高い低所得層は負担が大。IMFは低所得層に絞った所得移転が望ましい対応と指摘
 エネルギー・食料の供給ショックによる影響は国家間でも格差
― 先進国では、エネルギー供給ショックによる消費の下押し効果は低所得層で特に大きい
― 食料品への支出割合が高い新興国・低所得途上国では、食料供給ショックによる消費の下押し効果が相対的に大きい
8

 タイでは過去にも大規模洪水が発生。2011年には、日系自動車企業の部材調達が滞り、日本国内生産に影響した経験も

― ただし、10月中旬にかけ更なる大雨が予想されており、操業停止の長期化や被害地域の拡大には依然警戒が必要

日本：個人消費は耐久財特需が剥落。今後は良好な所得環境を背景に緩やかに拡大
12
 8月の消費活動指数は、耐久財特需の剥落などにより、前年比＋0.9％と伸びが鈍化
― 耐久財消費は春先の自動車特需の反動から減少。食品などの非耐久財も、根強い節約志向を背景に弱含みが継続
― 底堅く推移してきたサービス消費も、台風・大雨や猛暑による外出控えの影響などから拡大ペースが鈍化
 今後は良好な所得環境が消費を下支え。個人消費は緩慢なペースながら、底堅く推移する見通し
― 実質所定内給与はプラス圏の伸びを維持しており、基調的な所得環境は良好。増加基調の購買力が消費を下支え
― ただし、インフレ率の再上昇で消費の回復ペースは緩慢。マインドの改善が一服したことも回復ペースを抑える要因に

（注） 旅行収支要因は、非居住者による国内消費（インバウンド消費）を控除し、居住者に
よる海外消費（アウトバウンド消費）を加えるための調整要因。アウトバウンド消費は
SNAの家計最終消費支出に含まれるが、GDPの計算上は輸入として控除される
（出所） 日本銀行「消費活動指数」より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
消費活動指数（実質消費） 実質所定内給与の推移
（出所）厚生労働省「毎月勤労統計」、総務省「消費者物価指数」より、みずほ総合研究所
調査部作成

の伸びはプラス圏を維持

背景に弱含みが継続
耐久財特需の反動などから
実質消費の伸びが鈍化
自動車特需の
反動で減少
天候要因などで

日本：景気ウォッチャーは消費の緩やかな持ち直しを示唆も、家計・企業とも先行きには慎重
13
 家計動向（現状判断DI）は改善。9月の消費回復を示唆するも、回復ペースは緩慢
― 特にサービス関連の上昇が顕著。8月に増勢が一服したサービス消費は、大型連休や秋の行楽需要を受け持ち直し
― ただし、先行き判断DIは低下しており、個人消費全体でみれば緩やかな回復であることを示唆
 一方、企業動向は、現状・先行きとも慎重。景気回復の勢いは強くない
― 企業動向（現状判断DI）は製造業・非製造業ともに低下。AI関連など需要は堅調も、資材高などの供給制約が重石に

― 先行き判断についても、原材料高や金利上昇、中東情勢の不透明感などが重石となっている模様
（出所） 内閣府「景気ウォッチャー調査」より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

### Page 1

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
Recharging Growth: UPS Recovery and HVDC Potential; Initiate at Buy
CITI'S TAKE 
We initiate coverage on Voltronic with a Buy rating and NT$1,050 target 
price. Voltronic is a Taiwan-based UPS ODM, supplying to western power 
vendors including Schneider, Riello, Vertiv and Eaton. We view 2026E as the 
earnings trough, with improving online UPS mix and operating leverage 
driving 28% earnings rebound in 2027E. Structural outsourcing by western 
power vendors should support sustained share gains, while expansion into 
HVDC power racks offers a new stream for AIDC growth, supporting a 
potential re-rating. Our TP applies 25x 2027E EPS against earnings CAGR 
of 20% in 2026-2028E, broadly consistent with its L-T historical average.
Outsourcing and mix upgrade underpin the recovery — Western vendors’ 
increasing focus on integrated AIDC infrastructure strengthens the rationale for 
outsourcing select UPS platforms. Voltronic’s customization capabilities and 
flexible manufacturing position it to capture additional opportunities. A rising 
contribution from online and higher power UPS should support margins, driving EPS 
CAGR of 20% in 2026-2028E. 
Power racks could materially broaden its growth opportunity — Voltronic’s high 
voltage EV-charger experience and longstanding relationships with western power 
vendors support its entry into HVDC power solutions. It has progressed into 
customer sampling, with management indicating that HVDC power rack mass 
production could begin in 2H27E at the earliest. Successful commercialization could 
broaden its addressable market and support a valuation re-rating.
Earnings recovery and strong cash returns  — We view 2026E as the earnings 
trough, followed by a recovery in 2027-2028E. A richer online UPS mix and operating 
leverage should lift GPM from 27.1% in 2026E to 28.1% and 29.0% in 2027E and 
2028E, respectively. Its relatively asset-light business model, net cash position and 
high dividend payout should provide additional support for shareholder returns.
Initiate Buy with TP NT$1,050 on 25x 2027E EPS — Our target multiple is broadly 
consistent with Voltronic’s average forward P/E since 2013, supported by its 
competitive position in UPS outsourcing and durable customer relationships. 
Clearer evidence of power-rack commercialization could offer further re-rating.

### Page 4

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
13 October 2026 Citi Research
4
Executive Summary
We initiate coverage of Voltronic with a Buy rating and target price of NT$1,050
(25x 2027E EPS), supported by structural UPS outsourcing, improving product mix
and emerging opportunities in AI datacenter power infrastructure. Voltronic is a
Taiwan-based ODM of uninterrupted power supplies (“UPS”) and power
conversion products for global brands. UPS system provides backup electricity and

protection against power disturbances, helping critical equipment maintain
operation during supply disruptions. UPS accounted for 63% of Voltronic’s 2025
group sales. Its pure-play ODM model, customization capabilities and
longstanding customer relationships underpin its competitive positioning.
Our positive stance on the shares is based on three major reasons:
1. We see expansion into HVDC power racks as a potentially significant new
growth opportunity. Its experience in high-voltage EV chargers provides

relevant engineering capabilities, while its established relationships with
western power vendors support access to customer development programs.
Voltronic has progressed into customer sampling, with management
indicating that HVDC power rack mass production could begin in 2H27E at the
earliest. Successful commercialization could broaden its addressable market
and support a valuation re-rating, although meaningful earnings contribution
remains subject to qualification, design wins and production schedules.

2. Structural UPS outsourcing should support sustained market share gains.
Western power vendors are allocating more resources to integrated AIDC
infrastructure and energy management, strengthening the rationale for
outsourcing selected products. Voltronic’s engineering expertise, flexible
manufacturing and ability to handle a broad range of customized products in
small batches make it well positioned to capture these opportunities.
3. The shift toward online and higher power UPS should improve revenue quality

and profitability. We expect online UPS to be the principal driver of the
earnings recovery, with online UPS sales growing 12% CAGR in 2026-2028E as
outsourcing opportunities and demand for higher power systems expand.
We view 2026E as the earnings trough, followed by a recovery in 2027-2028E. We
forecast EPS of NT$33.00/NT$42.30/NT$47.25 in 2026E/2027E/2028E, implying
earnings growth of 28% and 12% in 2027E and 2028E. A richer online UPS mix and

operating leverage should lift GPM from 27.1% in 2026E to 28.1% and 29.0% in
2027E and 2028E, respectively. Its relatively asset-light business model, net cash
position and high dividend payout should provide additional support for
shareholder returns.
Our target price applies 25x forward P/E to 2027E EPS, broadly consistent with
Voltronic’s long tern average forward P/E since 2013. We believe its competitive
position in UPS outsourcing, durable customer relationships and manufacturing

capabilities justify this multiple, with clearer evidence of power rack
commercialization offering potential for further re-rating.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 8

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
13 October 2026 Citi Research
8
Investment Thesis
Positive #1 – AIDC creates a new growth runway, with
power-rack expansion potentially lifting Voltronic into
broader power solutions
We see Voltronic’s expansion into power racks as a potentially significant extension
of its AIDC opportunity in the medium to long term, enabling the company to
capture the broader share of datacenter power spending. We believe Voltronic’s
existing high-voltage engineering capabilities, established relationships with

western power vendors and customer collaboration provide a credible foundation
for winning business in HVDC power rack.
Rapid growth in AI workloads and rising rack density increase the power capacity
required, alongside more demanding requirements for efficiency, reliability and
load management. Nvidia’s roadmap targets 800VDC infrastructure supporting
racks of 1MW and beyond, while its latest architecture includes power racks that
supply 800VDC to compute racks within existing AC-powered facilities. We believe

this creates scope for greater power-infrastructure, even as the architecture
consolidates conversion stages to improve efficiency.
EV-charger experience provides a relevant technical foundation for HVDC
development
Voltronic already offers DC fast chargers ranging 30-240kW, with modular designs
delivering constant power across a 300-100VDC range and charging controls
developed internally. These products demonstrate existing experience with high-

voltage conversion, protection, thermal management and modular power-system
design. We believe these capabilities should support its development of 800VDC
PSUs and reduce the engineering learning curve.
Established westerns customer relationships strengthen its route to market
Voltronic’s UPS ODM business provides existing engineering relationships and
familiarity with customers’ quality, customization and manufacturing
requirements. Its pure ODM business model also emphasizes avoiding competition

with customers. We believe this positioning makes it a suitable partner for western
vendors who are expanding their AI infrastructure offerings. Existing relationships
should help Voltronic secure development opportunities and understand product
roadmaps early, although each new platform still requires qualification.
Voltronic has already taken concrete steps toward this opportunity. The company
has publicly introduced an HVDC PSU for power racks, while its broader

development plan extends into solutions combining battery backup unit (BBUs).
battery management systems (BMS), and DCDC converters through collaboration
with power-system companies.
Management indicated discussions with two Tier 1 vendors with the intended
solution ultimately servicing the service providers. This creates a potential path
from supplying individual power products to participating in a larger, integrated
power-rack offering.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Development has progressed into customer sampling, although specifications
continue to evolve with customers’ requirements. Management pointed out that
shipment of 800VDC products possible from 2H27E at the earliest.
We view its existing relationships with global power-solution vendors as a key
advantage, while its commitment to operating as a pure ODM without competing
against customers under its own brand supports this partnership model.

That said, customer qualification, confirmed design wins and mass-production
timing remain key milestones before we can confidently evaluate a material
contribution. Sales potential also depends on Voltronic’s scope of supply, which
determines content per rack and the share of system value it captures. Progress
toward commercial shipments could strengthen earnings visibility and support a
re-rating as Voltronic establishes its position in AIDC power infrastructure.

Positive #2 – UPS outsourcing provides a structural share-
gain opportunity

We see structural outsourcing as a durable growth driver for Voltronic, allowing its
UPS business to outgrow underlying industry demand. Rising investment in digital
infrastructure, commercial applications and industrial automation supports
continued demand for power protection, while established brands have incentives
to outsource selected hardware platforms.
Voltronic’s dedicated ODM model, engineering capabilities and manufacturing
efficiency position it to capture this opportunity. In our view, growth can come from

both an expanding market and the transfer of existing production from customers’
factories to qualified manufacturing partners.
UPS remains a structurally growing power-protection market
Global Market Insights estimates the worldwide UPS market at US$13bn in 2025,
rising to US$14.5bn in 2026E and US$26.8bn by 2035E, implying a 7.1% CAGR
over 2026-2035E. Although estimates vary by research provider and market
definition, the underlying drivers include increasing dependency on electronic

systems, growth in industrial automation and smart manufacturing, increasing
power outages and grad instability, rising costs of downtime and greater
requirements for reliable electricity.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 11

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
13 October 2026 Citi Research
11
Datacenters provide an important source of incremental demand, while
commercial and industrial applications broaden the market beyond the AI
investment cycle. Datacenters expansion supports demand for online, double
conversion UPS systems, which continuously condition incoming power and
protect critical equipment against supply disturbance. Commercial applications –
including offices, retail networks and healthcare facilities – require protection for

servers, communications equipment and other essential systems. Industrial
demand comes from protecting sensitive control, automation equipment and
critical processes where power interruptions can disrupt production.
The competitive landscape is led by established international suppliers. Per Global
Market Insights 2025 analysis, Schneider Electric, Eaton, Vertiv are the three
leading suppliers, with the top 5 collectively controlling approximately 60-65% of

the global UPS market. Other peers include Huawei, KSTAR, Kehua, Delta and
Riello.
This concentration matters for the outsourcing thesis – securing additional projects
from a relatively small number of large brands can open substantial manufacturing
opportunities.

Western brands’ strategic priorities support further selective outsourcing

Per our industry checks, major UPS brands still manufacture a substantial portion
of their UPS products internally, while increasingly moving toward energy
management and committing resources to broader AIDC infrastructure. This could
result in small and medium UPS products to be gradually outsourced as this
transition develops. We view this as a multi-year opportunity, particularly where
established platforms can be transferred to qualified ODMs while customers retain

control over product specifications, branding and distribution.
We identify three economic reasons for this shift –
1.Outsourcing can improve manufacturing and capital efficiency: A specialist
ODM can aggregate demand across customers, consolidate component
purchases and spread manufacturing overhead across a larger production
base. For brands, this can reduce incremental factory investment and simplify
capacity planning.
2.Outsourcing can accelerate product development: access to ODMs’

established platforms, component knowledge and manufacturing processes
allows brands to adapt existing designs for additional markets and
applications. Engineering resources can then concentrate on product
differentiation.
3.Broader system offerings create competing demands on vendors’ resources.
Leading UPS brands emphasize on monitoring, operational management and
integrated solutions, strengthening the rationale for outsourcing selected
hardware while allocating more resources to system integration, and software.

Voltronic’s dedicated ODM model, engineering capabilities and manufacturing
efficiency should position it to capture further UPS outsourcing

Voltronic’s ODM focus is evident from its commitment to operate without having a
brand of its own to compete with its customers. This aligns its interests with
customers and differentiates it from suppliers that combine branded sales with
contract manufacturing. Long-standing relationships with leading vendors,
including Schneider, Riello, Vertiv and Eaton, provide a foundation for securing

additional projects. Its engineering capabilities enable it to meet diverse product
specifications, while flexible manufacturing supports the small production batches
and broad model range characteristic of the UPS industry.
Its track record in product qualification and certification also creates barriers, as
moving established programs to other suppliers requires additional validation,
audits and testing. We believe this combination of customer trust, execution and

cost competitiveness positions Voltronic to secure more outsourcing orders,
including higher-power online UPS, as western brands allocate greater resources
to AIDC infrastructure and integrated power solutions.

Positive #3 – Online and high-power UPS mix upgrade
should support structurally better growth
We expect Voltronic’s shift toward online and higher-power UPS to become an
increasingly important driver of revenue growth and profitability. Moving up the

product range increases power capacity and engineering content per system,
supporting higher ASPs and greater differentiation. We forecast online UPS sales to
rise at a 12% CAGR in 2026-2028E, ahead of offline UPS at 6%, with online UPS
gross profit growing faster at 16% as product mix improves.
Offline UPS remains a stable revenue base, yet offers more limited differentiation.
Offline UPS typically serves home offices, small businesses and basic power

applications. These products feature relatively simple designs and lower costs.
We view this segment as increasingly mature and commoditized, with lower
technical barriers exposing suppliers to greater pricing pressure, including
competition from Chinese manufacturers. Scale, procurement efficiency and cost
control hence are the key competitive factors, while opportunities for sustained
ASP and margin expansion are more constrained, in our view. Our model assumes

offline UPS gross margin to be at 24-26% in 2025-2028E.
Online UPS offers greater technical differentiation and stronger margin potential.
Its double-conversion architecture continuously converts AC power into DC and
back into AC, providing stable voltage and frequency, avoiding power disturbances
and enabling zero transfer time to battery operation.
It is particularly suited to complex, power-sensitive applications such as
datacenters, industrial automation and precision control systems, where even brief

interruptions or power quality disturbances can disrupt operations. The business
also exhibits high-mix, low-volume characteristics, particularly in customized
Schneider, 18%

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)

commercial and industrial applications, where customers require different power
ratings, redundancy configuration and battery interfaces etc. Our model assumes
online UPS gross margin to be at 31-34% in 2025-2028E.
We believe this creates opportunities for an experienced ODM partner such as
Voltronic to differentiate through customization and reliable delivery, while sharing
development and procurement resources across customers and product platforms.

T ogether with demanding certification, customer qualification and reliability
testing, these capabilities raise entry barriers and switching costs, supporting
higher ASPs, strong customer retention and better margin potential as Voltronic
expands into higher power systems.
We project online UPS sales to increase 12% CAGR in 2026-2028E, lifting its
contribution to group sales from 37% in 2025 to c.44% in 2028E. The underlying
driver comes from a stronger outsourcing opportunity thanks to the AIDC boom.

Western vendors, including Schneider, Vertiv are expanding their focus on
integrated AIDC infrastructure, power distribution, and infrastructure
management. Meanwhile, rising power requirements within conventional AC-
based datacenters support demand for higher power UPS systems, prompting
vendors to expand UPS production capacity. We believe the combination of
stronger demand and competing resource requirements can strain available
engineering and manufacturing capabilities, increasing the appeal of qualified

ODM partners to supplement internal production. Voltronic’s customization
capabilities and flexible manufacturing position it to capture this opportunity.

Financial Analysis
Income statement – 2026E marks the earnings trough; online UPS drives
the recovery from 2027E
Voltronic delivered an 8.7% sales CAGR and 9.9% net profit CAGR over 2018-2025,
although earnings have become more volatile in recent years due largely to swings
in the inverter business. We forecast sales to decrease 1% y/y to NT$20.4bn in
2026E, before recovering 8% y/y and 7% y/y to NT$21.9bn and NT$23.4bn in

2027E and 2028E respectively. The key driver is the higher value online UPS.
We forecast online UPS sales to grow 10%/13%/10% y/y in 2026E/2027E/2028E,
lifting its sales contribution from 37% in 2025 to 44% by 2028E. T ogether with
offline UPS, we estimate UPS products to make up 72% of group sales in 2028E vs
2025’s 63%. Conversely, we forecast inverter sales to decline 27% y/y in 2026E and
remain broadly flat thereafter, reducing their sales contribution from 29% in 2025

to 18% in 2028E.
We expect gross margin to bottom at 27.1% in 2026E vs. 29.0% in 2025, reflecting
negative impact from material cost inflation and weaker inverter profitability.
Thereafter, we forecast gross margin to recover to 28.1% and 29.0% in 2027E and
2028E, supported by a richer online UPS mix and improving segment margins. We
estimate online UPS gross margin to increase from 30.8% in 2026E to 33.4% in
2028E driven rising contribution from high power online UPS, while its rising sales

mix could provide increasing contribution to group gross profits.
Operating leverage should further support profitability. Opex fell from 8.0% of sales
in 2025 to 7.4% in 2026E and could remain at 7.2% thereafter, while R&D
investment is maintained at 4.2% of sales. We therefore forecast OPM to recover
from 19.7% in 2026E to 20.9% and 21.8% in 2027E and 2028E.
We estimate net profit to decrease 18% y/y in 2026E, partly hampered by a larger
FX loss, before rebounding 28% y/y and 12% y/y in 2027E and 2028E. We forecast

EPS to register NT$33.00/NT$42.30/NT$47.25 in 2026E/2027E/2028E.
We attribute Voltronic’s earnings decline in 2025 and our forecast for a further
decline in 2026E to persistent inverter weakness and additional cost and supply
pressures. In 2025, adverse weather in Pakistan shortened the inverter peak
season and weakened demand, while intensifying Chinese competition pressured
pricing and profitability. The weakness extended into 2026E as the Middle East

conflict disrupted logistics and delayed shipments, alongside continued customers
destocking in Pakistan. In 1H26, RMB appreciation further weighed on margins.
Meanwhile, raw material shortages constrained shipments and higher metal and
key component prices eroded gross margins. We therefore forecast earnings to
decline 18% y/y in 2026E before improving 28% y/y in 2027E.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Balance Sheet – Asset light model and net cash suggest high returns
Voltronic maintains a relatively asset-light balance sheet with fixed assets making
up 24% of total assets in 2025 despite its sizable sales scale. We estimate the

company to remain in a net cash position throughout 2026-2028E. We forecast
cash and cash equivalent assets to account for 36-39% of total assets in 2026-
2028E while there are no meaningful short-term borrowings and long-term debt.
We forecast ROE to rise from 37% in 2025 to 40% in 2026E and 48% in 2027E. The
high ROE is not driven by financial leverage, but primarily by its asset-light
business model, strong operating margins and high capital distribution.

Voltronic also maintains high shareholder-return policy. We assume an 87% cash
dividend payout ratio through 2026-2028E, implying cash dividends of
NT$29/NT$37/NT$41 per share.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
We value Voltronic using a forward target P/E of 25x our 2027E EPS to derive a
target price of NT$1,050, broadly in line with its long-term average forward P/E
since 2013. We believe forward P/E is appropriate given the company’s established
earnings base and the importance of product mix and operating leverage to its
profitability. Using 2027E EPS captures a fuller year of anticipated earnings

recovery following the softer 2026E base, alongside the ongoing transition toward
online and higher power UPS. This also aligns our valuation with the earnings
outlook investors are likely to focus on as the recovery progresses.
Our target multiple of 25x is supported by Voltronic’s competitive position in UPS
outsourcing. Its established relationship with western power vendors and ability to
accommodate high mix, low volume production creates a solid foundation for the

intact share gains, supporting the durability of its earnings and hence justify a
valuation broadly consistent with its historical average. Stronger evidence of power
rack commercialization and sustained UPS mix improvement could support a
further re-rating.

Key downside risks to our Buy rating and target price include:
1.Slower UPS demand or outsourcing gain. Weaker commercial and industrial
spending, delayed datacenter projects or customer inventory adjustments
could weigh on orders.
2.  Less favorable product mix and margin pressure. A slower transition toward
online and high-power UPS, more aggressive pricing or higher component
costs could limit margin improvement.

3. Persistent PV inverter competition. Aggressive pricing by Chinese suppliers,
weaker residential solar demand or unfavorable subsidy changes could
pressure inverter sales and profitability.
Our quantitative model assigns a High Risk rating to Voltronic. However, we
believe such a rating is not warranted due to: 1) its established franchise and
longstanding relationships with leading power vendors, with demanding
qualification requirements and high customization supporting customer retention

and repeat business; 2) its track record of profitability and positive operating cash
flow enables internally funded investment and reduces reliance on external
financing; and 3) the company has a strong balance sheet and has been in net cash
position.

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)

Company Overview
Established in 2008 and listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange in 2014, Voltronic
Power is a Taiwan-based design and manufacturing service (DMS) provider
specializing in uninterrupted power supplies (UPS) and power-conversion
products. The company is headquartered in Taipei and operates manufacturing
facilities in Taiwan, China and Vietnam, with approximately 5,000 employees.

Voltronic adopts a pure-play ODM business model and does not market products
under its own brand. This avoids direct competition with customers and has helped
the company establish long-term relationships with most global Tier-1 UPS
brands, as well as numerous regional brand owners.
The company provides customers with product design, R&D, sourcing,
manufacturing and system integration services, with a focus on high-mix,
relatively low-volume and customized products.

Voltronic’s core products include offline UPS systems ranging from 400VA to 3kVA
and on-line UPS systems ranging from 1kVA to 3MVA. The company has
progressively expanded from conventional single phase UPS products into three-
phase, high-power and modular UPS systems. It has also diversified into off-grid
and hybrid solar inverters, energy storage systems, and EV chargers.
In 2025, online UPS contributed 37% of group sales, offline UPS 26%, inverters

29% and others 8%. By geo, Asia accounted for 60% of group sales, followed by
Americas 22%, EU 16% and others 2%.

Shareholder Structure
As of March 2026, founder, chairman and general manager Alex Hsieh was the

largest individual shareholder, holding 9.6% of outstanding shares. Based on
disclosed direct, family-related and investment company holdings, we estimate
the founder and related parties collectively controlled c.30% of outstanding
shares.
Key Management Team
nAlex Hsieh – Founder, Chairman and General Manager: Mr. Hsieh has led
Voltronic since its establishment in 2008. He holds a bachelor’s degree in
business management from Tatung University and previously served as

Chairman and General Manager of Centralion Industrial and as a director of
Phoenixtec Power. His extensive experience in the UPS industry underpins
Voltronic’s pure-play ODM strategy.
nMonica Wang – Vice General Manager: Ms. Wang joined Voltronic at its
establishment in 2008. She previously worked as a manager at Centralion
Industrial.
nChih-Hsin Chin – Vice General Manager: Mr. Chin joined Voltronic in 2008.
Before joining the company, he worked as a manager at Phoenixtec Power.

Voltronic Power
Company description
Established in 2008 and listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange in 2014,
Voltronic is a Taiwan-based design and manufacturing service (DMS) provider
specializing in uninterrupted power supplies (UPS) and power-conversion
products. Voltronic adopts a pure-play ODM business model, which avoids
direct competition with customers and has helped the company establish

long-term relationships with most global Tier-1 UPS brands. The company is
headquartered in Taipei and operates manufacturing facilities in Taiwan,
China and Vietnam.
Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on Voltronic, supported by an earnings recovery,
structural UPS outsourcing and emerging opportunities in AIDC power
infrastructure. Its customization capabilities and longstanding relationships
with western power vendors position it to capture additional outsourcing

orders and expand into higher power products. HVDC power racks offer a L-T
growth opportunity and potential re-rating catalyst. We view 2026E as
earnings trough, with a richer online UPS mix and operating leverage driving a
28% earnings rebound in 2027E.
Valuation
We value Voltronic using a forward target P/E of 25x our 2027E EPS to derive a
target price of NT$1050, broadly in line with its long term average forward P/E
since 2013. We believe forward P/E is appropriate given the company’s

established earnings base and the importance of product mix and operating
leverage to its profitability.
Risks
Key downside risks to our Buy rating and target price include: 1) Slower UPS
demand or outsourcing gain. 2) Less favorable product mix and margin
pressure. 3) Persistent PV inverter competition. 4) Weaker macro economy

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

個股聚焦  
2026/10/20

陳奕均 fion.chen@sinopac.com

泰合 (6467 TT) 
開花結果在即

永豐觀點 
抗血栓口溶膜貼片 BEQIA 歐美取證在即。 
 投資評價與建議 
投資建議買進：泰合 10/20 上市掛牌，掛牌價105.6 元。由於(1) 泰合開發之口溶
膜劑型藥物有效解決高齡與吞嚥困難之臨床痛點下，市場競爭力足；(2) 抗血栓口
溶膜貼片 BEQIA 歐美取證在即，2027 年在授權與上市權利金開始認列下 ，營運轉
虧為盈且呈大跳躍狀；(3) 由於多數學名藥廠受限專利訴訟，最快需至 2028 年 4
月後才可上市銷售，促使泰合享有約一年的「黃金市場空窗期」 ，能與原廠直接瓜
分市場。且因 BEQIA 屬 505(b)(2)新劑型新藥，即便2Q28 後學名藥陸續進場，其
獨特的劑型優勢仍能築起價格防護牆，所面臨的價折壓力相對輕微，建議買進，目
標價 170 元( 18 X 2027 EPS )。

營運現況與分析 
公司簡介：泰合生技成立於 2010 年，專注開發 505(b)(2)新劑型新藥與特殊學名
藥，核心技術為自主研發的 TDS 透皮／透黏膜傳輸平台，將現有口服藥品成分改
造為透皮與透黏膜藥物傳導劑型 ，提供方便使用且更安全的新劑型藥物。 產品開發
上，505(b)(2)部分，治療抗血栓口溶膜貼片(每日兩片)BEQIA 靜待歐美取證中外，
其餘則仍在臨床階段中，困難學名藥部分，化療止吐口溶膜2018 年日本取證銷售
中外，其餘四項藥品則在進行洽談授權合作。 
圖一：泰合產品線開發時程

資料來源：泰合；永豐投顧研究部整理，Oct. 2026

### Page 2

2

口溶膜劑型藥物能有效解決高齡與吞嚥困難之臨床痛點 ：傳統口服錠劑藥物必須經
過食道、進入胃腸道消化，再經由肝臟代謝後才進入血液循環，泰合開發之口溶膜
劑型藥物， 可讓藥膜在接觸舌頭或口腔黏膜數秒內快速溶解 ， 病患完全不需飲水即
可完成給藥， 且部份藥物分子能直接透過口腔黏膜上皮細胞微血管吸收， 直接進入
全身血液循環， 不僅能提高藥物生體可用率， 還能減少胃酸對藥物的破壞與對胃腸
道的刺激下，有效解決高齡與吞嚥困難之臨床痛點，提高患者服藥順從性。 
抗血栓口溶膜貼片 BEQIA 歐美取證在即，提前卡位下，營運動能可期：泰合自主
開發的抗血栓口溶膜新劑型新藥 BEQIA， 預計4Q26 取得歐盟藥證 、2Q27 獲美國
FDA 核准上市。目前該藥原廠在美國市場年銷售額>200 億美元，相較原廠口服錠
劑，泰合 BEQIA 具備三大競爭優勢：不需飲水（服藥便利）、解決吞嚥困難（減
輕照顧端壓力），以及攜帶與給藥便利（優化長照與醫療機構給藥流程）。 
市場競爭方面，由於多數學名藥廠受限專利訴訟，最快需至2028 年 4 月後才可上
市銷售，促使泰合享有約一年的「黃金市場空窗期」 ，能與原廠直接瓜分市場。此
外，由於BEQIA 屬 505(b)(2)新劑型新藥，即便2Q28 後學名藥陸續進場，其獨特
的劑型優勢仍能築起價格防護牆，所面臨的折價壓力應相對輕微。 
泰合海外市場營銷預計將採授權分潤模式，研究部預估 1H27 歐美地區簽約金應
有機會各達 500~1000 萬美金外，隨美國地區 2Q27 上市、通路鋪設及醫保陸續
准入，Peak sales 市佔率 2-3%(預期將落於 2029-30 年)、銷售分潤 12-15%估算
下，稅後 EPS 貢獻將落於 10-20 元。 
財務預估：預估泰合 2026 年營收 0.1 億元，稅後虧損 1.89 億元，稅後 EPS 以掛
牌後股本 5.99 億元估算，稅後 EPS-3.15 元，2027 年預估歐美地區授權簽約金各
500 萬美金及 1000 萬美金 ， 及上市銷售後權利金認列下 ，預估營收將跳升至7.75
億元，稅後淨利 5.68 億元，稅後 EPS 9.49 元。

## Document 162: 9月ＦＯＭＣ議事録：タカ派姿勢に滲む「データ依存」の慎重さ ～FRBは年内追加利上げを視野に入れるも、決定は今後の経済指標次第～

Source PDF: all report pdf/9月ＦＯＭＣ議事録：タカ派姿勢に滲む「データ依存」の慎重さ   ～FRBは年内追加利上げを視野に入れるも、決定は今後の経済指標次第～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-08
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

FED  Watching

2026 年 10 月 8 日

1/5 
9 月ＦＯＭＣ議事録：タカ派姿勢に滲む「データ依存」の慎重さ 
～FRB は年内追加利上げを視野に入れるも、決定は今後の経済指標次第～ 
 経済調査部 主任エコノミスト  桂畑 誠治  
（℡：050-5474-7493）

要旨  
・9 月FOMCでは全会一致（12 対 0）で 0.25%の利上げを決定。非投票メンバーを含む参加者 
全 19 人が利上げに賛成した。 
・経済・金利見通し（SEP）提出者の大半が年内（10 月または 12 月）の更なる利上げを見込 
んでおり、全体としてタカ派的トーンが際立つ。 
・インフレリスクは上振れ傾向にあるものの、中長期のインフレ予想は 2%目標水準に安定。 
FRBは毎会合のデータ次第で柔軟に判断する「オープンな姿勢」を維持している。 
・今後の見通しとしては、景気や労働市場が安定する中でコアCPIやコアPCEの鈍化が確認され 
れば、予防的な追加利上げが見送られる可能性がある（最終決定は 12 月FOMCまでのデータ次
第） 。

１． 9 月FOMC ：参加者 19 人全員 が利上げ に賛成  
ケビン・ウォーシュFRB議長の新体制下で初めての利上げが決定された 9 月FOMCの議事録が公表 
された。新体制では会合直後の声明文のテキスト量が大幅に削減されたほか、記者会見時間も短
縮され、直接的な情報発信量が絞り込まれている。そうした中で、後日公表される「議事録」の
情報量は引き続き高水準に維持されており、FRBの本音を推し量る最重要ツールとしての重みが増
している。ただし、公表がFOMC終了から 3 週間後となるため、情報の遅効性には注意が必要であ 
る。 
9 月の利上げ決定については、投票権のないメンバーも含めた参加者全員（19 人）が利上げに賛
成した。さらに、経済・金利予測（SEP：議長自身は今回も SEP を提出していない）で示されていた 
通り、SEP を提出した 18 人の参加者のうち大半が「年内（10 月 27-28 日または 12 月 15-16 日）に
更なる利上げが適切となる可能性が高い」と見解を示しており、インフレへの強い警戒感を示すタ 
カ派（金融引き締め寄り）なトーンが際立つものとなった。ただし、委員会として今後の政策判断 
は新たに入手されるデータに依存（Data-dependent）するスタンスを明確に維持している。

２．労働市場は完全雇用水準で安定、インフレには上振れリスク 
委員会は全会一致（12 対 0）で、FF金利誘導目標レンジを 0.25％ポイント引き上げ、3.75％〜

### Page 2

2/5 
4.00％とすることを決定した。労働市場が完全雇用に近い水準で安定している一方で、インフレ 
率が高止まりしていることが背景にある。 
インフレの判断では、参加者は共通して、 「インフレ率は高止まりしており、ここ数ヶ月でイン 
フレ抑制に向けた十分な進展は見られていない」と指摘した。 「原油や精製燃料製品の価格を押し 
上げた継続的な地政学的動向や、急増するAI関連投資がインフレ圧力に寄与している」との認識 
が示された。 
複数の参加者がコア財・コアサービス価格の上振れや、関税の影響に言及したほか、数名の参 
加者がPCE指数の算出手法改定による影響や、3 ヶ月年率換算のインフレ率が年後半にインフレを 
過小評価しやすい傾向に留意すべきだと警鐘を鳴らした。一方で参加者全員が、 「中長期のインフ 
レ予想に関する市場ベースおよび調査ベースの指標が、委員会の 2％目標と一致する水準を維持し 
ている」と判断しており、追加利上げを過度に急ぐ必要性は示されなかった。 
労働市場に関しては、参加者は概ね「労働市場の状況が安定している」と判断し、 「完全雇用に 
近い状態にある」と評価した。 「失業率が現在の水準付近にとどまり、労働市場の状況は引き続き 
安定する」と予想した。一方で、数名の参加者が低水準の採用・解雇率など流動性の低下を指摘 
し、一部の参加者がAI専門職における局所的な賃金上昇圧力に触れた。そのうえで、参加者は概
ね「労働市場に対する上振れリスクと下振れリスクはほぼ均衡している」と評価した。 
経済活動に関しても、参加者は一般に「堅調なペースで拡大している」と評価。 「地政学的動向 
による供給面での悪影響が強まったものの、旺盛な設備投資と底堅い個人消費が経済活動を支え 
ている」との判断が示された。なお、一部の参加者からは、AI関連投資が設備投資と潜在生産性 
を押し上げる一方で中期的に総需要が総供給を上回るリスクや、株高などを背景とする金融環境 
の緩和的状況、高所得層と低・中所得層における消費の二極化構造への懸念が指摘された。 
以上のように、参加者は共通して「労働市場が完全雇用に近く力強さの兆候も見られる一方で 
インフレ率は高止まりしており、経済活動が堅調なペースで拡大していること」を強調した。さ
らに、ほぼすべての参加者が、 「インフレリスクは上振れに傾いているものの、労働市場に対する 
リスクは減少し、現在はほぼ均衡している」と評価した。こうした経済見通しと変化するリスク 
バランスに基づき、全参加者（19 人）がFF金利誘導目標レンジの引き上げが適切であると判断し
た。

3．政策金利の水準感とリスク管理の姿勢 
今回の議論において注目されるのは、利上げの根拠と政策金利水準に対する見解の多様さであ 
る。多くの参加者は、予想以上の需要や供給面での悪影響によりインフレ率が持続的に目標を上 
回り続けることに対する保険（Insurance）を提供するため、誘導目標レンジを高めの軌道とする 
ことがリスク管理上の観点から賢明であると強調した。 
一方で、相当数の参加者はリスク管理上の観点からではなく、自身の標準的な見通し（Modal 
outlooks）に基づいて高めの目標軌道が必要であると見なした。さらに、数名の参加者は中立金 
利の推計値を引き上げたことに触れ、現在の政策金利は「緊縮的ではない、あるいは極めて緩や

当化する材料となる。 
また、バランスシート政策に関しては、数名の参加者が国債市場の円滑な機能を認めつつも、 
将来の市場不全に備えたツールやコミュニケーションの強化を提案したことが確認された。

４．先行きに対する見通し 
今回の会合以降の金融政策の見通しに関して、大半の参加者（SEP提出者 18 人のうち）が年末 
までにFF金利誘導目標レンジを再度引き上げることが適切となる可能性が高いとの見方を示し 
た。ただし、全員が共通のスタンスとしてオープンな姿勢で各会合に臨み、今後の決定は新たに 
入手する情報と、それが経済見通しおよびリスクバランスに与える含意に依存することを強調、
毎会合ごとに判断されることを示し、政策の自由度を維持した。 
今回の議事録は全体として「年内追加利上げ」に前向きなタカ派的トーンが目立ったものの、 
インフレ予想の安定や「毎会合データ次第」というスタンスから、FRBが無理な連続利上げを焦っ 
ているわけではないことが示された。今後の金融政策の焦点を左右するのは、10 月・12 月FOMCま
でに発表される景気、労働市場や、コアCPI、コアPCEの動向である。景気や労働市場の安定が続 
くと予想されるもとで、インフレの鈍化傾向が確認される場合、予防的な追加利上げは見送られ 
る可能性がある（最終決定は今後のデータ次第） 。

## Document 163: [000270] 기아(000270) 물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결기아(000270) 물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000270] 기아(000270) 물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결기아(000270) 물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결 - 기업.pdf

주가 추이

견조한 판매 확대로 매출 견조, 이익은 시장 기대치 하회 예상 
3Q26 동사 매출액과 영업이익은 각각 33.6조원(+17.3% 이하 Y oY)과 2.39조
원(+63.6%) 기록 전망. 3Q 매출액은 친환경차 중심 신차 확대 통해 두 자릿수 
성장세를 기록. 3Q 판매량은 도매 기준 84.3만대(+7.6% 이하 Y oY)로 전년 동
기 대비 성장 폭을 확대했고, 특히 친환경차 판매량 29.8만대(+45.9%)를 기록
하며 이번 분기 매출 성장에 절대적으로 기여.  
다만, 3Q 수익성은 4~5월 상승했던 원자재 투입 영향과 ‘경제형EV 믹스’가 더
욱 확대(13.2% → 14.5%)되면서 단기 수익성에 부정적 작용하며 시장 기대치 
2.5조원을 다소 하회할 전망. 그러나, 기말 환율이 1,356원/$ 수준으로 낮 아 지
면서 전년 동기 수 익 성 악화에 크게 영 향 을 미쳤던 판매보증비 외화환산손익 
영향이 개선(1.6%p 수준) 기대되며, 관세 부담 또한 북미 HEV 확대로 전년 동
기 대비 약 4,500억원(2.0%p) 감소하면서 부정적 수익성 영향 만회 전망.  
유럽은 점유율 확대, 북미는 수익성 개선으로 
올해 북미 HEV와 유럽 BEV 중심 신차 출시 및 물량 확대가 본격화되면
서, ’27년에는 이익 성장 기여도가 확대될 것으로 전망. 미국 조 지 아 텔 루 라 이
드 HEV 신규 생산(2.8만대) 및 HMGMA 스 포 티 지HEV신규 생산(1.0만
대) 등 수 요 가 높은 북미 현 지 에 서 HEV 물량 확대 중으로 4Q에도 텔루라이
드 HEV와 스포티지 HEV의 현지 생산/판매 확대를 비롯하여 셀토스 HEV 
출시로 ’27년 북미 중심 HEV 신차 물량 증가 및 믹스 개선 기대. 유럽은 강한 
BEV 수요가 지속되면서 중국 업체들의 공세 강화 중이나 동사의 물량 확대 또
한 견조. 3Q EV3 2.9만대, EV5 2.2만대 등 유럽 BEV 수요 대응 전략 주효. ’27
년에는 K4/셀토스 및 쏘렌토 등 HEV 신차 중 심 의 친환경차 PT 믹스 다양화
로 유럽 내 물량 확대 흐름 지속 전망 
물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결, 기말 배당 매력도 부각 
’27년 동사 영업이익 추 정 치 는 11.1조원(+13.5%)으로 증익 지속 전망. 최근 
환율 추세 고려 시 내년 수익성에 부 정 적 영향 확대 가능성 존재하나, 올해 확
인된 신차 중심 물량 확대를 통해 부정적 환율 영향을 상쇄할 것 으 로 기대. 
12M EPS 추정치 하향(-2.9%) 및 상 반 기 적 용 한 코스피 프리미엄 제거(-
10%)로 목 표 주 가 를 260,000원으로 하향. 그러나, RA 설립에서의 동사 지분 
참여 및 기말 배당(’26년 DPS 7,000원 추정)에 따른 상 대 적 배당 수익 매력
도 부각 등은 주가 모 멘 텀 에 있어 기대 가능한 요소.

이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 8

기아 (000270) [한화리서치]

8 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일)

## Document 172: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로긴 호흡으로 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로긴 호흡으로 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-09-29
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

(005930) 
삼성전자 
2026.09.29

반도체 손인준 
6154 / sij@eugenefn.com

긴 호흡으로 
 투자의견 STRONG BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 560,000원(유지) 
 현재주가 270,000원(9/28)

 삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익이 103.6조원(+16%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. 메모리 가격 등 전반적인 반도체 업황에 대한 기존 가정은 대체로 
유지했으나, 최근 원화 강세에 따라 원/달러 환율 가정을 1,440원에서 
1,400원으로 낮추고 MX 사업부의 적자 확대를 반영해 실적 추정치를 하
향 조정했다. 또한 2Q26 재고가 3Q26 매출로 인식되는 과정에서 발생하
는 추가 성과급 충당금 부담도 반영했다.  
 DS 부문의 3Q26 영업이익은 104.0조원(+17%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. 서버 DRAM과 eSSD 중심의 가격 인상이 이어지며, DRAM과 
NAND의 Blended ASP는 각각 +17%, +18%QoQ 상승할 것으로 추정한
다. 1cnm 수율 개선과 HBM4의 빠른 램프업도 실적 개선에 기여할 전망이
다. Foundry/LSI는 HBM4 베이스다이 생산 확대, Groq 3 LPU 양산 및 선
단 공정 가격 인상에 힘입어 하반기 매출 성장이 예상된다. 다만 성과급 충
당금과 감가상각비 부담으로 영업적자 축소 폭은 제한적일 것으로 보인다. 
 DX 부문의 3Q26 영업이익은 -1.8조원(적자지속qoq)을 기록할 것으로 추
정한다. 삼성전자는 메모리 가격 급등에도 제품 가격 인상을 제한하며 시장 
점유율 확대에 주력하고 있다. 이러한 가격 정책과 갤럭시 Z 폴드8 등 신제
품 흥행에 힘입어 판매량은 당초 예상치를 상회할 것으로 보인다. 다만 원
가 상승분의 전가가 제한되면서 영업적자 폭은 크게 확대될 전망이다.  
 삼성전자의 4Q26 영업이익은 114.9조원(+11%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. DRAM과 NAND의 Blended ASP는 각각 +11%, +12%QoQ 상승
하며 견조한 흐름을 이어갈 것으로 보인다. 환율 가정 조정에 따라 2026년
과 2027년 영업이익 추정치를 각각 365.2조원(+735%yoy), 595.8조원
(+63%YoY)으로 낮췄다. 4Q26~1Q27 범용 DRAM·NAND 및 2027년 
HBM 가격 협상 환경은 공급업체에 유리하게 전개되고 있어, 향후 가격 가
정의 추가 상향 가능성이 높다고 판단한다.  
 극심한 DRAM 공급 부족으로 인한 HBM 적층 단수 하향 조정(base die 수
요 증가), Memory Wall에 대응하기 위한 3D Stacked DRAM·zHBM 도입 
논의 등은 파운드리의 기술적 가치를 부각시키는 요인이다. 파운드리의 수
익성 회복 속도는 완만할 전망이지만, 메모리와 파운드리를 연계한 턴키 비
즈니스의 시너지는 AI 반도체 시장 내 경쟁력을 높이고, 메모리 시장 점유
율 확대에도 기여할 것으로 보인다. 적극적인 메모리 가격 인상 정책으로 
업계 최고 수준의 Blended ASP도 장기간 유지할 것으로 예상한다. 
 이제 메모리 업종 주가는 업황의 지속 가능성을 확인하고 꾸준한 주주환원
을 반영하며, 중장기적으로 점진적인 상승세를 이어갈 것으로 판단한다. 삼
성전자는 메모리·파운드리를 아우르는 턴키 역량과 가격 정책에 기반한 실
적 차별화를 통해 피어 그룹 내 경쟁력을 높여갈 것으로 보인다.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

1,417,000 
제약업종

◼ 3Q26 파업 영향에도 컨센서스 상회 
◼ 4Q26 지연 물량 인식 및 신규 공장 기여로 실적 개선 전망 
◼ 투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
목표주가는 DCF 방식으로 산출(WACC 8.1%, 영구성장률 3.5% 적용). 기
존 공장의 높은 가동률을 기반으로 5공장 매출 기여 확대, 미국 생산거점 
확보, 신규 모달리티 진출을 통한 중장기 성장 지속 전망.

3Q26 Review: 파업 영향에도 컨센서스 상회  
3Q26 매출액 1조 4,903억원(+18.5% yoy, +12.8% qoq), 영업이익 6,401
억원(+1.1% yoy, +9.1% qoq), 영업이익률 42.9%(-7.5%p yoy)를 기록하며 
당사 추정치 및 시장 컨센서스 상회. 5월 1차 파업에 따른 매출 차질(약 
1,500억원) 에도 불구하고, 기존 1~4공장 Full 가동이 지속되며 견조한 실
적 달성. 5공장 PPQ 배치 생산 확대와 및 미국 록빌 공장 매출 인식도 외
형 성장에 기여. 다만, 신규 생산시설 초기 가동비와 제품 믹스 영향으로 
영업이익률은 전년 동기 대비 하락.

4Q26 매출액 1조 5,764억원(+22.6% yoy), 영업이익 7,075억원
(+34.0% yoy) 전망  
4Q26에는 2분기 파업으로 지연된 생산물량의 순차적인 매출 인식과 미국 
록빌 공장 매출 기여, 5공장 PPQ 배치 생산 확대로 실적 개선 예상, 동사
는 신규 생산시설의 매출 가시성 확보를 바탕으로 2026년 매출 성장률 
20%를 무난히 달성할 것으로 전망(연초 가이던스 15~20%).

신규 수주 회복과 포트폴리오 확장에 주목 
미국 의약품 관세정책 불확실성 완화로 지연됐던 수주 협상이 재개되며 신
규 수주 환경 개선 중. 3Q26 약 3억달러의 신규 수주를 확보했으며, 대형 
계약 협상도 구체화되고 있어 2027년 수주 회복 기대. 이는 5공장 가동률 
확대와 중장기 성장 가시성을 높이는 요인. 6공장은 ‘26년말~’27년초 착
공을 목표로 기존 증설 일정 유지. 
폴리펩타이드 그룹 인수는 1차 공개매수 응모율 87.88%를 기록하며 연내 
인수 완료 추진 중. 인수 완료 시 펩타이드 CDMO로 사업영역이 확대되며 
글로벌 생산거점 및 고객 기반 다변화 기대.

구분 Buy(매수) Marketperform(중
립) 
Underperform(매도) 
비율 93.2% 6.8% 0.0% 
산업 투자의견 
- Overweight(비중확대) 
  : 향후 6개월간 업종지수상승률이 시장수익률 대비 초과 상승 예상 
- Neutral(중립) 
  : 향후 6개월간 업종지수상승률이 시장수익률과 유사한 수준 예상 
- Underweight(비중축소) 
  : 향후 6개월간 업종지수상승률이 시장수익률 대비 하회 예상

기업 투자의견 
- Buy(매수)  
  : 향후 6개월간 시장수익률 대비 10%p 이상 주가 상승 예상 
- Marketperform(시장수익률)

### Page 2

2 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Oil Price Dynamics Remain Key to the Outlook
Chief Investment Office, Macro Strategy Team
Inflation, interest rates and equity market dynamics have changed materially since mid-
August, as widening attacks on Middle East and Russian crude oil and refining capacity
worsened supply and price conditions. Indeed, with the oil market already strained, an
early-September attack on a major Saudi Arabian export pipeline sharply raised concerns

about a protracted and deepening oil crisis. While Middle East crude exports eventually
managed to significantly rebound, increased security risks and freight costs raised
physical crude prices.
Against this backdrop, the spot Brent price surged from $92/barrel in mid-August
back into a $115 to $135/barrel price range until recently. Reflecting unusual market
tightness, other North Sea oil grades were recently trading even higher, up to $160/
barrel, according to the International Energy Agency (IEA). Even as it cooled from $135

to $115/barrel between October 2 and October 20, spot Brent is still almost double
year-ago levels, and so are many diesel prices. The AAA average U.S. retail diesel price
recently hit a new record and was 74% higher year-over-year (YoY) as of October 20.
With insufficient regional supply, the New York Harbor wholesale diesel price is 130%
higher from a year ago, according to the Energy Information Administration (EIA).
Indeed, sharply higher oil prices and a growing diesel shortage have put the global diesel

market under extreme strain. The U.S., for example, has little distillate cushion and
limited spare refining capacity. The distillate inventory (diesel and fuel oil) has decreased
from 37 days of demand coverage in January to just 26 days by early October, according
to the EIA. This 30% drop brought the supply buffer to the bottom of its historical
range. With limited refining capacity and reliance on imports for about 10% of regional
consumption, East Coast distillate inventories are the lowest since 1990, for example,

and are expected to remain below their 5-year average into late 2027, according to EIA
data. More broadly, according to the IEA’s October Oil Market Report (OMR), “oil market
balances are forecast to remain in deficit through the end of the year, with constrained
middle distillate supplies likely to persist well into 2027.”
Thus, although wage growth/labor demand do not appear inflationary, the oil crisis
risks continuing to feed “core” inflation. According to the Federal Reserve’s Beige Book 1

covering precisely the mid-August to early-October period of heightened oil-market
turmoil, “higher oil prices permeated all District reports,” with firms noting that fuel
surcharges were passing on increased costs throughout the supply chain. The 22% rise
in airfares over the past year alone contributed around 0.2 percentage point (pp) to the
2.6% 12-month increase in the “core” consumer price index (CPI) for September. Aside
from transportation costs effects, the spike in diesel prices also portends high food

prices with a few-month lag given planting/harvesting cost considerations.
By solidifying expectations for higher Fed interest rates to fight inflation, the surge in oil
prices has contributed to the spike in long-term Treasury yields this year. According to an
October 13, 2026, Empirical Research Partners report, the Iran-war oil crisis is estimated
to have contributed about 0.50 pp to the roughly 2.40 pp increase in the real U.S. long-
term Treasury rate from 2020 to early October (Exhibit 1A). According to the report, this

oil-price effect accounts for “much of the rise in real rates since February 27, 2026...”
On the positive side, while most other underpinnings for higher rates compared to the
pre-pandemic decade are structural (Exhibit 1A), the oil-price-related effect on interest
rates is reversible, implying that a resolution to the current oil crisis has the potential to
help lower both inflation and interest rates, relieving some of the current headwinds to
growth and asset prices.

Indeed, while much of the increase in interest rates since the pandemic reflects stronger,
more normal economic growth, and has thus been accompanied by surging corporate
1 Summary of Commentary on Current Economic Conditions by Federal Reserve District, Federal Reserve, October 2026.
Investment Implications
Oil market conditions remain a swing
factor for the outlook. A shift lower in
oil risk premia and improvement on
the refining front would go a long way

toward easing inflation and interest
rates while bolstering risk-asset
appetite. A gradual increase in Fixed
Income portfolio duration continues to
make sense, in our view.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
earnings and risk-asset prices, worsening oil-market conditions since mid-summer and
associated spike in yields have been accompanied by a loss of momentum in U.S. equity
prices (Exhibit 1B). Given their higher sensitivity to energy price and interest rates shocks,
Small-caps lost relatively more momentum than Large-caps during this period.
The Large-cap rally also faded, though, as oil and interest rate headwinds widened stock-

level return dispersion, with roughly 40% of constituent stocks in bear market territory
(down 20% or more). Still, buoyed by the Technology sector as well as by expectations
of conflict resolution, the market-capitalization weighted S&P 500 Index has mainly
consolidated just below its peak. While the equal-weighted S&P 500 Index stumbled more,
it was just about 3% off its peak as of October 20.
While the economy remains resilient and profit expectations remain strong, persistent

oil market turmoil makes the macro backdrop more fragile. For example, the Beige Book
not only highlights concern about spreading fuel-related inflation, as noted above, but its
language also suggests that economic activity lost some momentum since its mid-July
to late-August report. Overall, it describes growth as slight to moderate, with generally
moderate manufacturing growth except for robust activity in the energy-heavy Dallas
District. Little growth is reported in other sectors overall, with most Districts seeing little

to no change in consumer spending, for example.
Employment was described as stable to little changed, with generally adequate labor supply
except for certain specialized trade workers. Five of twelve Districts note some reports of
businesses cutting staff through attrition, delaying hiring or anticipating less job creation
as a result of AI-driven productivity gains. Most Districts reported that business maintained
a positive outlook for coming months, though “expectations tend to be for slight growth,

weighted toward manufacturing and burdened by persistent uncertainty.”
The September employment and retail sales reports seem to validate the anecdotal Beige
Book evidence. Also, the 0.4% annualized manufacturing production growth over the July to
September period (and 1.1% YoY, according to the Federal Reserve Broad), while welcome,
is hardly an indication of booming conditions. Against a backdrop of rising electricity prices,
wage growth lagging inflation, and higher gasoline prices broadly offsetting this year’s tax

cut benefits, real consumer spending is unlikely to accelerate much absent stronger labor
demand and real wage growth. A resolution to the oil crisis that brings economic relief and
tempers interest rates is thus critical for continued expansion in 2027, in our view.

Investment Implications
Portfolio maintenance is a key
component of successful investing.
It’s time for investors to assess their
portfolios—or look under the hood.
MARKET VIEW
Six Questions Investors Should Be Asking Themselves Right Now
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
After four years of rising equity prices, investors have plenty to celebrate. Despite

inflation scares, geopolitical tensions, trade wars, rising interest rates and breathless
predictions around AI, the S&P 500 has returned some 131% (total returns) since the
bull market in equities commenced in October 2022. Corporate earnings hav e generally
exceeded expectations; AI has ignited a historic investment boom, and America’s
technology giants have powered the market to new heights.
We believe the bull will rumble on into 2027. We also believe that now is the time for

investors to take stock of their portfolios, since bull markets have a way of changing the
composition of portfolios. In addition, investors should not assume that the investment
strategies that worked well in the past four years will necessarily deliver comparable
results during the next four. Below are six questions investors should be asking
themselves right now:
Number One: How concentrated is my portfolio? Many investors believe they are
invested in the stock market when, in fact, they are invested in a handful of companies—

specifically AI-related names that have soared and dominated the index over the past
few years. Information technology now accounts for roughly 40% of the market cap of
the S&P 500, while the top 10 companies represent a share of 38.4%.
As the Financial Times recently noted, U.S. equity and credit markets have become so
increasingly concentrated in AI-related groups that “more than tw o-thirds of companies
in the Russell 1000 index of large U.S. groups are linked to AI, either directly or

indirectly or because the technology is becoming embedded in their business models.” 2
Meanwhile, Big Technology hyperscalers and companies that are beneficiaries of the AI
spending boom make up about 16% of the U.S. high-grade bond market after a huge
borrowing binge, making the grouping the largest in the investment-grade bond market. 2
The upshot: Investors that have been rewarded by concentration need to ask themselves
what percentage of their wealth is tied to the all-encompassing AI trade and consider

what would happen if mega-cap tech leadership falters.
Number Two: How diversified is my portfolio? While concentration asks whether
investors own too much of a few assets, diversification asks whether investors own enough
of different assets that behave differently when economic cycles or financial conditions
change. A portfolio containing hundreds of U.S. Equities via exchange-traded funds, mutual
funds and other assets may appear diversified. But if those companies are overwhelmingly

exposed to the same economic cycle, interest rate environment, or technology theme,
the diversification benefits are diluted. Case in point: While we remain constructive on
Emerging Market (EM) Equities, investors need to understand that a significant driver of
EM returns has come from AI-driven markets like Taiwan and South Korea.
Today, true diversification means considering exposure to hard assets (commodities, real
estate), EM mining and agricultural leaders, EM bonds and currencies, China bio-technology,

Small-cap international, and Private Equity and Hedge Funds for qualified investors.
Number Three: How tax-efficient is my portfolio? Bull markets create wealth—in
addition to tax liabilities. The good news is that after four years of rising equity returns,
investors are sitting on sizable, unrealized capital gains. The bad news: Market gains
can feel like a financial straitjacket, making it difficult to rebalance portfolios without
triggering significant tax liabilities.

Against this backdrop, now is the time for investors to review where the assets are
held—distinguishing between taxable and tax-advantaged accounts, and the attendant tax
ramifications. The appropriate mix depends on individual circumstances, including future
2 “Investors seek shelter from heightened exposure to AI”, The Financial Times, October 6, 2026.

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
withdrawal taxes. It is also time to consider tax-loss strategies, charitable donations of
securities and other tactics and strategies since in the end, the measure of inv estment
success isn’t simply how much money investors make. It’s how much they keep.
Number Four: Is it time to take profits?* There is a peculiarity to bull markets: The
more money investors make, the more the attendant tax liabilities. Tax liabilities, in turn,

can discourage selling, and the reluctance to sell can leave investors dangerously exposed
to yesterday’s leaders and compound concentration risks. That brings up an emotionally
difficult decision: When should investors sell their leaders or take some chips off the table?
The question is notably relevant today, given four years of substantial Equity gains.
Beyond tax considerations, investors need to understand that profit-taking can create
financial flexibility. Realized after-tax gains can replenish cash reserves, fund near-

term spending needs, reduce leverage or most importantly, provide capital to rebalance
portfolios in other areas of the market. In general, the goal of taking profits isn’t to get
out of the market—it is to ensure that after years of accumulated gains, these proceeds
are redeployed and redistributed across various asset classes that continue to support
and underpin long-term wealth accumulation.
Number Five: How well is my portfolio positioned for a new interest rate

regime? For much of the past quarter century, investors operated in a world of copious
liquidity, declining interest rates and relatively cheap capital. The era of capital abundance
favored stocks over bonds, but that era is over—we are shifting to a world of capital
scarcity, or intense competition for capital and the attendant hike in the cost of capital.
In this world, higher rates weigh the most on long-duration assets. Positioning for this
regime favors companies with strong balance sheets and cash flow over firms with heavy

debt or refinancing needs. High-quality bonds are more attractiv e in this environment;
sovereign debt risks are concentrated more overseas (i.e., France) than the U.S.
Number Six: How well is my portfolio positioned for the next shock? Shocks
happen. If the past four years have taught investors anything, it is that the unexpected
should be the expected. This decade alone has produced gut-wrenching market mo ves
related to a global pandemic, a ground war in Europe, the reconfiguration of global

supply chains, epic technological change and a war in the Middle East. Through it all, U.S.
Equities have trended higher but hardly in a linear fashion.
The next shocks could emerge in the global bond market, where mounting go vernment
debt and higher borrowing costs have triggered mounting volatility. It could emerge from
disappointing returns on massive AI investments. Or it could come from a geopolitical
event, another energy shock or any other unforeseen event. Investors can’t predict the

next shock, but they can prepare for it. So now is the time for investors to stress test
their portfolios against a potential shock, examining how a steep downdraft in Equities
or spike in bond yields would affect their retirement plans, liquidity needs and other
financial goals and objectives.
Exhibit 2: Six Questions to Stress Test Your Portfolio.
The six questions What investors should examine Portfolio diagnostic
1. How concentrated am I? Mega-cap technology exposure and overlapping holdings % of portfolio in top 10 stocks

2. Am I truly diversified? Asset allocation, geographic mix and correlated exposures Current equity/bond mix versus target
3. How tax-efficient am I? Unrealized gains, asset location and tax-loss harvesting Embedded capital gains and estimated tax cost of rebalancing
4. Is it time to take profits? Portfolio drift, oversized winners and valuations Positions exceeding target allocation
5. Am I ready for higher interest rates? Duration, leverage, refinancing risks and cash flows Bond duration and exposure to highly leveraged companies

6. Am I prepared for the next shock? Liquidity, downside exposure and portfolio resilience Estimated loss in a 20% equity selloff

### Page 6

6 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THE TICKER TAPE
What are rising credit spreads signaling about the broader market environment?
Christopher Hyzy, Chief Investment Officer
Rising French bond yields with a sharp rise in spreads above German bunds, mixed
with AI buildout concerns, are creating nervousness across the capital markets. This
nervousness centers around the ability to fund deficits, the potential for slower growth

and the future return on the announced capital investment. We expect these concerns
to continue to linger well into next year as growth worries remain in France, bond
yields stay sticky around current levels, and, in the case of AI, investors demand more
compensation for financing.
At this stage, we do not expect the rising spreads in targeted areas to be a precursor
for a material slowdown in economic growth overall. Rather, we believe this is a natural

signal that investors are moving up the quality stack as we move into the middle of
the AI buildout. The bottom line is that rising credit spreads in lower quality corners of
the market are natural along the lifecycle of financing. When free cash flow is called
into question, or when debt financing costs begin to approach the expected return on
investment, investors begin to reprice risk.
We continue to emphasize higher quality balance sheets in corporate and sovereign

credit as well as higher quality leaders in the AI buildout overall. The closer we get to
2027, investors will likely begin to transition from too much exposure to the “buildout”
and not enough exposure to “AI adopters.”

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
SS&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

Consumer Price Index is a statistical measure that tracks the average change over time in the prices paid by urban consumers for a representative "market basket" of everyday goods and
services.
S&P 500 Market-cap Weighted Index is a float-adjusted market-capitalization-weighted index, meaning a company's influence and percentage weight in the index are determined by its
total market value (share price multiplied by the number of outstanding shares).

S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Index is a variation of the traditional S&P 500 where every single company in the index is given the exact same weight—0.2%—regardless of its size or market
capitalization.
S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Industrials Index imposes equal weights on the index constituents included in the S&P 500 that are classified in the GICS® industrials sector.
Russell 1000 Index is a stock market index that tracks the performance of the 1,000 largest publicly traded companies in the United States.

or sale of any security or investment strategy. Merrill offers a broad range of brokerage, investment advisory and other services. There are important differences between brokerage and

of America, Merrill or any affiliate to sell or provide, or a specific invitation for a consumer to apply for, any particular retail financial product or service that may be available.
The Chief Investment Office (“CIO”) provides thought leadership on wealth management, investment strategy and global markets; portfolio management solutions; due diligence; and solutions
oversight and data analytics. CIO viewpoints are developed for Bank of America Private Bank, a division of Bank of America, N.A., (“Bank of America”) and Merrill Lynch, Pierce, Fenner & Smith

The Global Wealth & Investment Management Investment Strategy Committee (“GWIM ISC”) is responsible for developing and coordinating recommendations for short-term and long-term
investment strategy and market views encompassing markets, economic indicators, asset classes and other market-related projections affecting GWIM.
BofA Global Research is research produced by BofA Securities, Inc. (“BofAS”) and/or one or more of its affiliates. BofAS is a registered broker-dealer, Member SIPC and wholly owned subsidiary

of Bank of America Corporation.
All recommendations must be considered in the context of an individual investor’s goals, time horizon, liquidity needs and risk tolerance. Not all recommendations will be in the best interest
of all investors.
Asset allocation, diversification and rebalancing do not ensure a profit or protect against loss in declining markets.
Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.
Nonfinancial assets, such as closely held businesses, real estate, fine art, oil, gas and mineral properties, and timber, farm and ranch land, are complex in nature and involve risks including total

## Document 284: FX Daily_ Dollar has lost its safe-haven crown

Source PDF: all report pdf/FX Daily_ Dollar has lost its safe-haven crown.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-23
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The yen and Swiss franc have emerged as the safe havens of
choice as the greenback continues to show some vulnerability
to the AI story. A further widening in French spreads has not
materially hit the euro, but that correlation may spike again
soon. Today, PMIs are published, but focus will remain primarily
on global equities, France and oil

Frantisek Taborsky, Francesco Pesole and Chris Turner
The dollar’s vulnerability to the AI story is driving safe-haven demand
elsewhere, notably towards the yen and Swiss franc
USD: Losing safe-haven appeal
Yesterday was a rough session for risk sentiment, as global equities
sold oﬀ on a combination of higher oil prices and AI-related
concerns. FX moves conﬁrmed that the dollar is far from the
preferred safe haven at this stage, with the yen and Swiss franc
emerging as the main beneﬁciaries of the defensive rotation.
Crucially, the negative correlation between bonds and equities is
reasserting itself, and FX markets are taking their cues from that
dynamic. 10yr Treasuries broke back below 5.0% for the ﬁrst time

### Page 2

in a month, showing ever-reduced sensitivity to higher oil prices.
The same dynamic is visible at the front end, where the 2yr SOFR
swap rate dropped 15bp to 4.50% yesterday. That suggests equity
jitters are enough to dent market conviction on a large Federal
Reserve tightening cycle.
Still, the bar for pricing out a December Fed hike appears fairly high,
which should prevent larger USD corrections for the moment. Any
stabilisation in risk sentiment can help USD consolidate across

most pairs and take some shine oﬀ JPY and CHF. But the FX reaction
function to further risk sell-oﬀs is now looking much clearer.
On the data side, focus will be on S&P Global PMIs today.
Consensus is for some softening in the US ﬁgures, which, however,
remain deep into expansionary territory.
Francesco Pesole
EUR: Sensitivity to France can return
rapidly
The OAT-Bund 10yr spread closed at 174bp after touching 178bp
yesterday. That wasn’t the only EU sovereign spread widening

yesterday; bunds outperformed (+9bp) on haven demand, and
energy prices played a role too.
But OATs saw the only increase in 10yr yields in Europe (+6bp), and
the OAT-BTP spread has reached a grim 50bp milestone. The
budget/political situation is not visibly deteriorating, so the move is
probably a function of OAT’s simply having a higher beta to global
factors and perhaps some concerns about another weekend of
social unrest in France.
The euro’s sensitivity to France has clearly diminished, with the

common currency not materially underperforming G10 peers
yesterday. That link may, however, re-establish very rapidly should
a new pain level – perhaps 200bp – be breached. We aren’t
expecting that much further widening for now, but the risks are
clearly there, and the euro remains rather unattractive across the
board.

### Page 3

EUR/CHF remains the cleanest way to play European sovereign risk
at the moment, while EUR/USD should be quick to re-test the 1.1120
14 October low should bond turmoil persist. Today’s PMIs will
probably play a secondary role to France, which faces a Moody’s
credit review, and global equities. Expectations are for slightly softer
numbers compared to September.
Francesco Pesole
CEE: Global risk-off sentiment weighs on
region currencies
The end of the week oﬀers little in the regional calendar, with the

primary driver remaining the broader global narrative. Deepening
"risk-oﬀ" sentiment and the continued rise in energy prices are
weighing on emerging market currencies, including those in CEE.
Nevertheless, the region is demonstrating admirable resilience;
despite some ﬂuctuations in rates, the FX market remains relatively
robust. Even so, we maintain a bearish outlook on CEE currencies in
general. EUR/CZK and EUR/RON have reached the targets we
discussed here on Monday.

In the Czech Republic, we saw an interview yesterday with Czech
National Bank Deputy Governor Jan Frait, marking the start of the
current round of oﬃcial commentary ahead of the November
policy meeting. Although the market interpreted the interview as
dovish, we view it as preparation for a rate hike. Furthermore, as
Frait is one of the more dovish members of the Bank Board,
subsequent comments from other oﬃcials are likely to be more
hawkish. This supports further strengthening of the koruna, despite

a signiﬁcantly negative global environment; consequently, we
could see a test of the 24.200–24.250 range.
EUR/RON has normalised, returning to the 5.340 level, following the
parliament’s vote of conﬁdence in the new technocratic
government. As we noted previously, we do not expect the new
government to trigger an FX rally, but rather to foster stabilisation
around current levels. This remains our baseline view, though the
risk remains tilted to the upside for EUR/RON should political

uncertainty return.
